# Feature checklist

**765 implemented · 430 implemented but untested · 6 pending · 1201 tracked capabilities.**

Each capability appears exactly once. Completing it moves the same stable ID from Pending to Implemented. A setting or catalog entry alone does not complete a feature. Source references describe the implementation; validation limits are documented in [Testing](docs/TESTING.md).
New code that has not been built or tested belongs to Implemented — untested, with unchecked boxes representing pending verification. It is excluded from the verified implementation count. Each regression report applies only to its documented source checkpoint.

Maintained in `docs/feature-catalog.json`. Regenerate with `pwsh -File tools/Sync-PublicFeatures.ps1`; CI rejects an outdated checklist.

- [x] **F0104** Nested, ordered and linked-segment chapter playback, alternate editions/displays and editing chapters inside media containers. — MatroskaInspector reads bounded EBML Info/Chapters/SeekHead, discovers up to 500 same-folder candidates and rejects duplicate IDs/stale sources. Metadata fixtures include an unknown-size Cluster with SeekHead; actual FFmpeg Matroska extraction passes. Main-player bounded/EOF sequencing and XML/new-copy MKV editing retained. History is bounded to 1,000 local file stamps; batch rename relocates records.

Previous/next section and quick repeat verified only by tests.

### Audio

- [x] **F0033** Artwork and title/artist/album/genre metadata. — MediaService.Audio, MainViewModel.Audio, EqualizerPresets and media tools
- [x] **F0034** Metadata editing through LibVLC for formats that support saving tags. — MediaService.Audio, MainViewModel.Audio, EqualizerPresets and media tools

- [x] **F0036** CUE sheet chapters and native chapter navigation. — MediaService.Audio, MainViewModel.Audio, EqualizerPresets and media tools
- [x] **F0037** Native ten-band equalizer with preamplifier and 30 presets, alongside optional extended layouts. — Native ten-band equalizer, presets and output-profile integration; captured PCM verifies flat bypass level and gain/compensation at 31.25 Hz and 1 kHz.
- [x] **F0038** Equalizer gain compensation; extended layouts estimate the combined filter response. — Native combined-response headroom and acknowledged effective preamp; captured PCM at 20 Hz, middle centers and 20 kHz, including 192/384 kHz inputs. This is not a peak limiter.
- [x] **F0039** Migration of presets saved by the earlier localized interface. — MediaService.Audio, MainViewModel.Audio, EqualizerPresets and media tools
- [x] **F0040** ReplayGain track/album options, preamplification and peak protection. — MediaService.Audio, MainViewModel.Audio, EqualizerPresets and media tools
- [x] **F0041** Output device selection, audio tracks and delay, with native Original/mono/stereo/swap/left/right channel routing. — MediaService.Audio and SettingsPanel; captured left/right tone components and six-channel PCM downmix, native acknowledgement, startup, paused settings changes, output selection, source changes and persistence. Speaker layouts are tracked separately.
- [x] **F0042** Native adaptive RMS playback normalization and linked dynamic compression. — AudioEffects / native adaptive linked RMS leveling and existing compressor: actual quiet/loud PCM converges toward the configured RMS target; maximum boost, silence gate and sample protection are bounded. Settings are validated and saved before live publication. RMS normalization is not EBU R128/LUFS or a true-peak certification. Sources: docs/PLAYBACK_AUTOMATION.md; FluxPlayer.Tests/MoreThirtyChecks.cs.
- [x] **F0043** Optional FFmpeg conversion to FLAC, WAV, MP3, Opus, Vorbis, AAC, ALAC and WavPack. — MediaService.Audio, MainViewModel.Audio, EqualizerPresets and media tools
- [x] **F0044** Sequential conversion, progress, cancellation and optional loudness normalization. — MediaService.Audio, MainViewModel.Audio, EqualizerPresets and media tools
- [x] **F0045** Conversion writes to a temporary file and refuses to overwrite an existing destination. — MediaService.Audio, MainViewModel.Audio, EqualizerPresets and media tools
- [x] **F0046** Independent native 20-band logarithmic and 31-band ISO graphic equalizers, with requested gains and preamp within ±20 dB. — Native/FluxDsp, NativeDspBridge and dynamic EqualizerWindow; measured processed PCM, high-rate precision, live layout switching, persistence and visual panel review. Filter-only measurements: docs/NATIVE_DSP.md.
- [x] **F0047** Saved volume, full 10/15/20/31-band EQ layout, preamp, gain compensation and audio delay per output, with recall when selecting an output or starting the player. — AudioOutputProfile / MainViewModel.AudioProfiles / AudioOutputProfilesWindow; independent endpoint persistence, live native delay/volume, mute, boost limit, opt-out, startup, failure isolation and manual headphone-profile review. Full native layout/profile recall and unrelated-settings reload are covered by the multiband scenario.
- [x] **F0048** Low-pass headphone crossfeed with adjustable amount and cutoff. — AudioEffects / native Effects. Captured left-only PCM verifies filtered opposite-ear content; live native acknowledgement verified. Stereo only; disabled by default.
- [x] **F0049** Verified gapless transitions and crossfade. — RealtimeAudioSession / MainViewModel.Realtime / RealtimeAudioWindow: explicit 1–32-file main-queue PCM stream, bounded prebuffer, pause/resume/seek/stop, sample-adjacent gapless and measured live crossfade overlap. Actual silent WASAPI queue transition has zero underruns. Synthetic WAV continuity tested; compressed codec padding, queue repeat and dynamic append are not certified. docs/REALTIME_AUDIO.md.
- [x] **F0050** Advanced DSP chain and measured output quality matrix. — PcmAudioChain hosts up to 32 ordered effects and trusted managed DLL plugins through IPcmAudioEffect; actual separate assembly processing tested. Matrix/gain/delay signal checks cover six rates and five layouts in nonuniform frame blocks. Actual endpoint inspection records 60 exact format negotiations per device, separately from physical output certification. Existing sixteen native effects, EQ and meters retained. VST and universal device certification are outside this managed ABI. docs/REALTIME_AUDIO.md and docs/NATIVE_DSP.md.
- [x] **F0051** Online lyric providers and provider integrations. — OnlineLyrics + OnlineLyricsWindow: explicit HTTPS LRCLIB search/metadata matching, reviewed results, synchronized session apply and new LRC/TXT exports. ContinuationChecks.Lyrics verifies encoded Unicode search, invariant-duration exact matching, response bounds/parsing, 404/429/error/cancellation behavior and safe exports. CompletionChecks.NativeUi renders the actual panel in dark and light themes. LRCLIB only; no automatic attachment or plugin framework. FluxPlayer.Tests.
- [x] **F0098** Create and edit CUE sheets with track titles, artists, sources, ordering and 75-fps start positions. — CueSheet.Write / CueSheetEditorWindow; frame and multi-file regression tests, plus manual edit/save verification with original preservation.
- [x] **F0099** Preserve and edit advanced CUE directives such as CD-TEXT, PREGAP and additional indexes. — CueDocument / AdvancedCueWindow; preserves CD-TEXT, gap/flag/index/REM/unknown directives and whitespace, validates exact 75-frame timing and rebases FILE/CDTEXTFILE references for new UTF-8 copies. Unicode/BOM/Windows-1252, bounds, original/source preservation, cancellation and no-overwrite checks pass. Basic editor redirects potentially lossy imports. Desktop Tools launch, synthetic document loading and three-track validation reviewed. No burning, de-emphasis, silence synthesis or gapless playback is implied.
- [x] **F0105** Independent native 15-band ISO graphic EQ from 25 Hz to 16 kHz, with factory presets and complete output-profile persistence. — Native multiband module and EqualizerLayout; measured PCM at 25 Hz, 1 kHz and 16 kHz, flat/gain/headroom checks, live layout round trip, persisted 15-band profiles and visual review of all controls.
- [x] **F0108** Local synchronized LRC editor with timestamp insertion, session preview and new-copy UTF-8 export. — LyricFile, LyricsEditorWindow and session generation guards; validated timestamps, limits, Unicode, offset overflow, no overwrite/cancellation, native preview and source reset; desktop Tools access and Unicode editing reviewed.
- [x] **F0116** Explicit conversion sample rate, mono/stereo channels, lossy bitrate and WAV/FLAC PCM depth controls. — ConversionOptions / ConversionService / ConverterWindow; actual WAV/FLAC/MP3/AAC/Opus rates/channels, 16/24/32-bit PCM, MP3 bitrate, normalization interaction, duration and source preservation measured. Encoder-specific selection validation runs before process launch. Desktop Tools launch and lossless/lossy option enablement reviewed. Uses optional FFmpeg only for explicit conversion; live playback output rate/depth remain pending.
- [x] **F0132** Ten-band parametric EQ with independent frequency, gain and Q, alongside the existing graphic EQ. — AudioEffects / Native FluxDsp / AudioEffectsWindow. Captured PCM verifies a +6 dB peaking response; finite bounds, settings persistence, live updates, paused-state preservation and graphic-EQ coexistence verified.
- [x] **F0133** Configurable bass shelving filter. — AudioEffects / native AddBass. Captured 50 Hz/4 kHz stereo fixture verifies low-frequency boost and high-frequency level retention.
- [x] **F0134** Linked peak-envelope compressor with threshold, ratio, attack, release and makeup gain. — Native Effects / AudioEffectsWindow Dynamics. Actual captured loud-tone PCM proves gain reduction. This replaces the earlier ineffective option-only compression path; automatic loudness normalization remains separate and pending.
- [x] **F0135** Linked instantaneous sample-peak limiter with adjustable ceiling. — Native Effects. Captured PCM proves the -12 dBFS sample ceiling. No look-ahead or true-peak limiting is claimed.
- [x] **F0136** Stereo balance with independent left/right attenuation. — Native Effects. Captured hard-left fixture verifies right-channel attenuation and retained left level.
- [x] **F0137** Individual gain for decoded logical channels 1-8. — Native Effects / Channel controls. Stereo PCM proves -12 dB on one channel without changing the other. Bounds -24 to +12 dB. Complete physical surround mapping is not asserted.
- [x] **F0138** Individual delay for decoded logical channels 1-8. — Native Effects / Channel controls. Actual impulse response verifies 100 ms left delay while preserving the right-channel impulse. Bounds 0-200 ms; physical speaker assignment remains output-dependent.
- [x] **F0139** Mid/side stereo width enhancement, including mono fold-down. — Native Effects. Captured left-only PCM verifies changed side amplitude and stereo width. Range 0-2; stereo only.
- [x] **F0140** Echo with adjustable delay, feedback and wet amount. — Native Effects / Time effects. Actual impulse capture verifies the delayed repeat. Bounded feedback and native time buffers.
- [x] **F0141** Short multi-tap reverb with wet amount and decay controls. — Native Effects / Time effects. Actual impulse capture verifies the multi-tap tail; this is a compact room effect, not convolution reverb.
- [x] **F0142** Flanger with modulated short delay, rate, depth and wet controls. — Native Effects / Time effects. Captured PCM differs from bypass and remains finite.
- [x] **F0143** Chorus with longer modulated delay, rate, depth and wet controls. — Native Effects / Time effects. Captured PCM differs from bypass and remains finite.
- [x] **F0144** Stereo center voice reduction through common-center subtraction. — Native Effects / Tone and stereo. Captured correlated center tone is canceled. Centered instruments can also be removed; this is not AI stem separation.
- [x] **F0145** Inspectable audio signal path with actual native input/output peak and RMS meters. — AudioPathWindow / NativeDspBridge protocol 2. Native block meters and applied configuration sequence are verified against actual PCM. Metering runs only while the window is open and precedes output-device/mixer volume.
- [x] **F0188** Editable native logical-channel mixing matrix. — AudioEffects.Matrix / NativeDspBridge protocol 3 / fluxdsp.c: bounded finite 8x8 coefficients, live output-row/input-column matrix, identity default, actual stereo PCM channel-swap verification and saved settings. Up to eight decoded logical channels; physical speaker mapping remains pending. Sources and operational limits: docs/PLAYBACK_AUTOMATION.md. Verification: FluxPlayer.Tests/MoreThirtyChecks.cs.

### Library and spoken media

- [x] **F0122** RSS 2.0 and Atom podcast subscriptions with explicit feed refresh, enclosure browsing, episode search and listening state. — SpokenMediaService / PodcastDocuments / SpokenMediaWindow. Local HTTP RSS/Atom and redirect fixtures verify metadata, relative enclosures, deduplication, preserved progress, malformed input and atomic write failures. Desktop subscription and episode browser reviewed. No directory discovery, paid-feed authentication or background polling.
- [x] **F0123** Explicit cancellable podcast episode downloads and offline playback with profile-local copies and safe removal. — SpokenMediaService.Download / RemoveDownload and MainViewModel.Spoken. Exact downloaded bytes, cache reuse, streaming cancellation/temp cleanup, ownership checks and native decoding with the HTTP server disconnected verified. Each download is limited to 512 MiB and 30 minutes. No automatic download queue or network-wide codec guarantee.
- [x] **F0124** Nested OPML podcast subscription import and new-copy export, with URL deduplication and bounded XML parsing. — PodcastDocuments.ReadOpml / WriteOpmlCopy. Nested outlines, document round trip, URL validation, prohibited DTD, wrong roots, size limits and existing-copy preservation verified. Imports subscribe without requesting feeds; refresh is explicit.
- [x] **F0125** Local audiobook shelf with individual progress and speed, named bookmarks, restart and completion state. — SpokenMediaService / MainViewModel.Spoken / SpokenMediaWindow. Restart persistence, bookmark operations and source preservation verified; native playback proves explicit resume, speed, pause checkpoints and end-of-media completion. Desktop shelf/bookmark navigation reviewed. Progress saves at transport checkpoints; multi-file book grouping and online metadata are not included.

### Library and statistics

- [x] **F0126** Listening statistics grouped by artist, source or UTC day, with retained-history periods, completion counts and safe CSV copies. — ListeningStatistics / ListeningStatisticsWindow. Audio-only grouping, case merging, date windows, summed playing duration, completion counts, CSV quoting/formula protection and no overwrite verified. Desktop totals and recording-disabled notice reviewed. Statistics use retained opt-in session history and do not measure audibility.

### Audio and playback

- [x] **F0128** Attach a local external audio track to active media and select it through the existing audio-track controls. — MediaService.Picture / MainViewModel.Picture / MainWindow.ExtendedMedia. Native Matroska/WAV fixture verifies a new selected audio stream, preserved paused position, resumed transport, duplicate-attachment suppression and clearing on the next source. Audio settings and curated context access. Format, duration and synchronization remain decoder/source dependent.

### Video and picture

- [x] **F0129** Validated pixel-rectangle video crop with reset, current-source scope and matching exported snapshots. — VideoCrop / VideoPictureWindow / MediaService.Picture / VlcWpfRenderer / MediaService.Snapshots. Actual displayed color pixels, rejected out-of-source rectangles, 80x180 cropped snapshot and source reset verified. WPF maps a viewport of existing shared frame memory without a per-frame copy; native geometry is the fallback for other presenters. No automatic black-bar detection.
- [x] **F0130** Up to 32 named picture presets for brightness, contrast, saturation, hue and gamma, with temporary preview and persistent activation. — VideoPicture / MainViewModel.Picture / SettingsService / VideoPictureWindow. Actual dimmed/restored pixels, all native adjustment float controls, saved activation, Unicode names and failed-save isolation verified. Desktop sliders and context-menu access reviewed. Neutral disables the adjustment filter; non-neutral processing has a cost.
- [x] **F0154** Select either eye of a side-by-side stereo source with optional half-resolution aspect compensation. — VideoViewport / VlcWpfRenderer / snapshots. Actual blue-eye pixels and aspect-correct exported snapshot verified. Single-eye presentation; no stereoscopic display hardware output.
- [x] **F0155** Select either eye of a top-and-bottom stereo source with optional aspect compensation. — VideoViewport / VlcWpfRenderer. Source row coordinates and actual lower-half green/blue pixels verified. Presentation maps source coordinates to the current decoded frame dimensions.
- [x] **F0156** Bounded 1-8x zoom and horizontal/vertical pan inside the current picture. — VideoViewport / VideoViewportWindow. Geometry boundaries, actual panned pixels, snapshot agreement and source reset verified. Shared frame-memory viewport avoids an extra per-frame crop copy.
- [x] **F0157** Vertical picture flip with matching exported snapshots. — MediaService.Viewport / VlcWpfRenderer / snapshots. Actual exported green bottom-left appears at top-left after vertical flip. Shares existing orientation actions and transforms.

### Playback and sessions

- [x] **F0131** Named saved playback sessions containing ordered queue labels, selected occurrence, position, speed, repeat and shuffle, with explicit restore or restore-and-play. — PlaybackSessions / MainViewModel.Sessions / PlaybackSessionsWindow. Duplicate labels, missing-source remapping, staged position, durable state, invalid/conflicting saves and failed-write isolation verified. Native restoration respects autoplay-off and resumes only on explicit play. Desktop Restore queue verified without starting playback. Session limits: 100 saved, 10,000 entries each, 100,000 in total.

### Audio measurements

- [x] **F0146** On-demand source RMS analysis with measurement export. — AudioAnalysis / AudioAnalysisWindow. Actual FFmpeg astats measurements, safe versioned JSON copies and no-overwrite verified. Media tags remain unchanged.
- [x] **F0147** On-demand estimated true-peak analysis in dBTP. — AudioAnalysis / FFmpeg loudnorm input_tp. Independent source measurement parsed and verified from generated audio. Offline analysis, not playback true-peak limiting.
- [x] **F0148** Calculate source track ReplayGain and track peak. — AudioAnalysis / FFmpeg replaygain. Real measurement parsing/export verified. Calculation does not write tags or enable live normalization.
- [x] **F0149** Bounded onset/correlation BPM estimation with heuristic confidence. — AudioAnalysis.EstimateTempo. Synthetic periodic fixtures at 60/90/120/180 BPM and silence rejection verified. Uses first 120 seconds, 45-210 BPM; rhythmic aliases and uncertain results remain possible.

### Audio conversion

- [x] **F0150** Bounded parallel batch audio conversion with unique destinations and cancellation. — BatchConversion / ConverterWindow. Four duplicate-source jobs produce unique decodable outputs with exactly two active workers. UI allows 1-4 workers; completed copies survive cancellation.
- [x] **F0151** Join ordered local audio files into a new WAV or FLAC copy. — MediaProduction.JoinAudio / MediaProductionWindow. Two actual sources produce a 16-second 48 kHz stream; overwrite refusal and source preservation verified. 2-32 inputs decoded/resampled to stereo; no bitstream concatenation claim.

- [x] **F0163** Ordered virtual playback segments. — VirtualEditCursor / HandleVirtualEnd: declared segment order, end-of-source continuation and final Stop; actual native sequence checks and idempotent cursor tests. - [x] **F0164** Declarative virtual-edit script import and new-copy export. — VirtualEditStore / VirtualEditsWindow: versioned bounded JSON, source-stamp verification, no executable commands, no replacement of existing exports, validated round trip and malformed edit rejection. - [x] **F0173** Recurring weekday playback, stop, volume and alarm tasks. — PlaybackSchedule: local weekday/time and time-zone persistence, next occurrence calculation, skipped invalid/ambiguous DST times, durable claims, cancellation and interrupted-series recovery. - [x] **F0174** Deterministic priorities for simultaneous scheduled tasks. — PlaybackSchedule.ClaimDue: equal due times sort by priority -10 to +10, then stable ID; higher priorities dispatch last. - [x] **F0175** Scheduled immediate playback-volume changes. — ScheduledPlaybackAction.SetVolume / MainViewModel.Scheduling: bounded target volume, active fade cancellation and normal mute semantics; actual native VM dispatch is verified. Sources and operational limits: docs/PLAYBACK_AUTOMATION.md. Verification: FluxPlayer.Tests/MoreThirtyChecks.cs.
- [x] **F0176** Dismissible playback alarms with five-minute snooze. — PlaybackAlarmWindow / AlarmFired: owned nonmodal notice and system sound, Dismiss or a new one-time snooze task, preserving recurring-series state. Due alarm dispatch and durable task semantics verified. Sources and operational limits: docs/PLAYBACK_AUTOMATION.md. Verification: FluxPlayer.Tests/MoreThirtyChecks.cs.

### Metadata and files

- [x] **F0183** Previewed metadata extraction from filename patterns. — TagAutomation: escaped anchored filename patterns with title/artist/album/genre/track tokens, regex timeout, per-file preview, stale-source guard and actual metadata write/re-read. Decoded PCM remains unchanged. Sources and operational limits: docs/PLAYBACK_AUTOMATION.md. Verification: FluxPlayer.Tests/MoreThirtyChecks.cs.
- [x] **F0184** Previewed automatic batch track numbering. — TagAutomation.Numbering: explicit file order, start/increment and 0-65535 bounds; reviewed apply preserves unrelated tags and decoded PCM in actual file tests. Sources and operational limits: docs/PLAYBACK_AUTOMATION.md. Verification: FluxPlayer.Tests/MoreThirtyChecks.cs.
- [x] **F0185** Previewed metadata casing transformations. — TagAutomation.Case: title/sentence/upper/lower invariant casing for selected text fields, per-source current/proposed preview and actual write/re-read with unrelated-field and PCM preservation. Sources and operational limits: docs/PLAYBACK_AUTOMATION.md. Verification: FluxPlayer.Tests/MoreThirtyChecks.cs.
- [x] **F0186** Previewed Latin metadata diacritic and ligature transliteration. — TagAutomation.Transliterate: Latin accent/ligature conversion, selected fields, reviewed current/proposed values and actual metadata verification. Other scripts are preserved; this is not universal phonetic transliteration. Sources and operational limits: docs/PLAYBACK_AUTOMATION.md. Verification: FluxPlayer.Tests/MoreThirtyChecks.cs.
- [x] **F0187** SHA-256-assisted repair of moved library sources. — LibraryFingerprints / LibraryService.Relink: explicit prior hashing, size-filtered folder search, unambiguous unchanged-byte matches, reviewed atomic library/collection relink and queue/recent/resume/bookmark relocation. Loaded files are refused. Independent saved playlists/sessions remain separate. Sources and operational limits: docs/PLAYBACK_AUTOMATION.md. Verification: FluxPlayer.Tests/MoreThirtyChecks.cs.
- [x] **F0189** Reviewed media extraction and queue opening from bounded local ZIP archives. — ArchiveMedia / ArchiveMediaWindow: bounded end-record and central-directory preflight, safe flattened new-copy extraction, stale-source/expansion guards, cancellation cleanup and explicit queue playback. Actual extracted WAV decodes through LibVLC. ZIP only; no ZIP64/encryption/all-archive claim. Guide: docs/LOCAL_MEDIA_GUIDE.md. Tests: FluxPlayer.Tests/LocalMediaChecks.cs.

### Audio processing

- [x] **F0231** Windowed-sinc FIR impulse design for offline audio filtering. — FirDesigner / FirDesignerWindow: mono IEEE-float WAV generation, Hamming-windowed low/high/band-pass taps and reference-frequency gain normalization. docs/UNTESTED_PROCESSING_THIRTY.md. Acceptance passed on 2026-10-28: HundredFeatureChecks (F0231), docs/hundred-feature-results.json; scope and limits: docs/VERIFIED_HUNDRED.md.
- [x] **F0232** Offline impulse-response convolution into a new audio copy. — AudioLab.Graph / AudioLabWindow: explicit local IR input, mono/resampled first-ten-second impulse and afir coefficient gain. No live convolution or room-calibration claim. docs/UNTESTED_PROCESSING_THIRTY.md. Acceptance passed on 2026-10-28: HundredFeatureChecks (F0232), docs/hundred-feature-results.json; scope and limits: docs/VERIFIED_HUNDRED.md.
- [x] **F0233** Selectable cubic, Blackman-Nuttall and Kaiser resampling filters for offline audio. — AudioLab.Resample: swresample filter_type and 64-tap setting in the export graph. docs/UNTESTED_PROCESSING_THIRTY.md. Acceptance passed on 2026-10-28: HundredFeatureChecks (F0233), docs/hundred-feature-results.json; scope and limits: docs/VERIFIED_HUNDRED.md.
- [x] **F0234** Configurable 16-bit rectangular/triangular dither during offline export. — AudioLab.Quantize / AudioLabWindow: none, rectangular, triangular and high-pass triangular methods at the output quantizer. docs/UNTESTED_PROCESSING_THIRTY.md. Acceptance passed on 2026-10-28: HundredFeatureChecks (F0234), docs/hundred-feature-results.json; scope and limits: docs/VERIFIED_HUNDRED.md.
- [x] **F0235** Noise-shaped 16-bit dither for offline audio copies. — AudioLab.Quantize: Shibata, low-Shibata and Lipshitz selection, with sample-rate restrictions and base-dither replacement. docs/UNTESTED_PROCESSING_THIRTY.md. Acceptance passed on 2026-10-28: HundredFeatureChecks (F0235), docs/hundred-feature-results.json; scope and limits: docs/VERIFIED_HUNDRED.md.
- [x] **F0236** Two-pass processed sample-peak normalization. — AudioLab.Export: first-pass float astats sample-peak measurement, bounded gain calculation and second-pass quantized new-copy output. Not true-peak/loudness mastering. docs/UNTESTED_PROCESSING_THIRTY.md. Acceptance passed on 2026-10-28: HundredFeatureChecks (F0236), docs/hundred-feature-results.json; scope and limits: docs/VERIFIED_HUNDRED.md.
- [x] **F0237** Independent offline pitch shifting by semitones. — AudioLab.Graph: sample-rate pitch factor, resampling and compensating tempo stages, bounded to plus/minus twelve semitones. docs/UNTESTED_PROCESSING_THIRTY.md. Acceptance passed on 2026-10-28: HundredFeatureChecks (F0237), docs/hundred-feature-results.json; scope and limits: docs/VERIFIED_HUNDRED.md.
- [x] **F0238** Offline tempo changes independent of pitch selection. — AudioLab.Graph: 0.5–2 tempo multiplier combined with pitch-compensation atempo stages. docs/UNTESTED_PROCESSING_THIRTY.md. Acceptance passed on 2026-10-28: HundredFeatureChecks (F0238), docs/hundred-feature-results.json; scope and limits: docs/VERIFIED_HUNDRED.md.
- [x] **F0239** RIAA reproduction equalization for appropriate raw recordings. — AudioLab.Graph / cleanup group: explicit aemphasis RIAA reproduction option. Does not detect whether a recording already has RIAA applied. docs/UNTESTED_PROCESSING_THIRTY.md. Acceptance passed on 2026-10-28: HundredFeatureChecks (F0239), docs/hundred-feature-results.json; scope and limits: docs/VERIFIED_HUNDRED.md.
- [x] **F0240** Offline DC and subsonic cleanup. — AudioLab.Graph: explicit two-pole 20 Hz high-pass before pitch, convolution and output quantization. docs/UNTESTED_PROCESSING_THIRTY.md. Acceptance passed on 2026-10-28: HundredFeatureChecks (F0240), docs/hundred-feature-results.json; scope and limits: docs/VERIFIED_HUNDRED.md.
- [x] **F0241** On-demand source FFT spectrum and peak-envelope analyzer. — SonicAnalysis / SpectrumDrawing / SpectrumInspectionWindow: bounded mono PCM, Hann FFT snapshots, relative-bin-power plot, envelope, centroid, RMS and tempo estimate. Not a calibrated live output analyzer. docs/UNTESTED_PROCESSING_THIRTY.md. Acceptance passed on 2026-10-28: HundredFeatureChecks (F0241), docs/hundred-feature-results.json; scope and limits: docs/VERIFIED_HUNDRED.md.
- [x] **F0291** Offline bass management with main-speaker high-pass and LFE redirection. — ChannelMastering.Graph: two-pole crossover, averaged main-channel low-frequency sum plus existing LFE, explicit 40–200 Hz cutoff. Electrical file processing, not room calibration. docs/UNTESTED_MASTERING_THIRTY.md. Acceptance passed on 2026-10-28: HundredFeatureChecks (F0291), docs/hundred-feature-results.json; scope and limits: docs/VERIFIED_HUNDRED.md.
- [x] **F0292** Explicit named speaker layouts for multichannel file processing. — ChannelMastering.Speakers / ChannelMasteringWindow: mono, stereo, 5.1(side) and 7.1 normalization/output with named speaker reference. Acceptance passed on 2026-10-28: HundredFeatureChecks (F0292), docs/hundred-feature-results.json; scope and limits: docs/VERIFIED_HUNDRED.md.
- [x] **F0293** Independent per-output-channel mastering gain. — ChannelMasteringOptions.GainDb / Graph: minus 60 to plus 12 dB per physical output after permutation, with separate global headroom. docs/UNTESTED_MASTERING_THIRTY.md. Acceptance passed on 2026-10-28: HundredFeatureChecks (F0293), docs/hundred-feature-results.json; scope and limits: docs/VERIFIED_HUNDRED.md.
- [x] **F0294** Independent speaker delays in multichannel file exports. — ChannelMasteringOptions.DelayMs / Graph: bounded 0–2,000 ms delays for each physical output channel after routing/gain. No automatic distance measurement. docs/UNTESTED_MASTERING_THIRTY.md. ### Diagnostics

- [x] **F0271** Bounded external-control event journal with new-copy export. — BUILT WITH CURRENT SOURCE — ACCEPTANCE NOT VERIFIED. LocalControl / ExternalControlWindow: in-memory event inspection and explicit no-overwrite JSONL export. Does not claim a complete engine trace. Verification deferred at user request. docs/UNTESTED_EXTERNAL_MEDIA_THIRTY.md.; Individual local acceptance: FluxPlayer.Tests acceptance suites, docs/LOCAL_BACKEND_ACCEPTANCE.md (scope and limits).
- [x] **F0308** Bounded excerpt luminance analysis with new-copy JSON report. — VideoFinishing.AnalyzeLuminance / SaveReport: up to sixty reduced 8-bit YAVG samples from first sixty seconds, frame-average min/mean/max and source identity. Not HDR peak or nits. docs/UNTESTED_MASTERING_THIRTY.md. Acceptance passed on 2026-10-28: HundredFeatureChecks (F0308), docs/hundred-feature-results.json; scope and limits: docs/VERIFIED_HUNDRED.md.

### Playback

- [x] **F0272** Session-activated automatic media profiles by extension. — FluxPlayer.Tests/PersonalExperienceAcceptanceChecks.cs; individual functional results in docs/local-completion-test-results.json, with actual controls/native runtime, real isolated servers or live public metadata as scoped in docs/LOCAL_SESSION_ACCEPTANCE.md.
- [x] **F0283** Session-armed television recording schedules. — BUILT WITH CURRENT SOURCE — ACCEPTANCE NOT VERIFIED. TelevisionLibrary.Claim / TelevisionRecorder: future time or EPG programme scheduling, persisted claims, one capture at a time, missed-start handling and explicit arm/disarm. App must remain running. Verification deferred at user request. docs/UNTESTED_EXTERNAL_MEDIA_THIRTY.md.; Individual local acceptance: FluxPlayer.Tests acceptance suites, docs/LOCAL_BACKEND_ACCEPTANCE.md (scope and limits).

### Streaming and sources

- [x] **F0279** Local IPTV-channel navigation and explicit live-stream playback. — BUILT WITH CURRENT SOURCE — ACCEPTANCE NOT VERIFIED. TelevisionWindow: selected user-provided channel sources passed to the existing playback queue/engine. No provider subscription or DRM integration. Verification deferred at user request. docs/UNTESTED_EXTERNAL_MEDIA_THIRTY.md.; Individual local acceptance: FluxPlayer.Tests acceptance suites, docs/LOCAL_BACKEND_ACCEPTANCE.md (scope and limits).
- [x] **F0284** Opt-in segmented television buffering with buffered playback. — BUILT WITH CURRENT SOURCE — ACCEPTANCE NOT VERIFIED. TelevisionBuffer / TelevisionWindow: FFmpeg stream-copy MPEG-TS segments in owned session folders, nominal ten-second boundaries, completed-segment selection and bounded-retention/disk guards. Not seamless DVR or timeshift-server compatibility. Verification deferred at user request. docs/UNTESTED_EXTERNAL_MEDIA_THIRTY.md.; Individual local acceptance: FluxPlayer.Tests acceptance suites, docs/LOCAL_BACKEND_ACCEPTANCE.md (scope and limits).

## Pending (6)

### Playback and formats

- [ ] **F0031** Exhaustive codec/container/hardware compatibility matrix. — PlaybackCompatibility / PlaybackCompatibilityWindow now run a bounded actual local fixture matrix with separate requested decoder policy, observed opaque surface, presented video frames and decoded PCM callback counts. Six audio and five video codecs across none/any/d3d11va/dxva2 policies pass 44 valid combinations; four malformed-file combinations fail as expected on the installed NVIDIA/runtime. Untested codecs, containers and hardware remain uncertified. docs/NATIVE_GPU_PRESENTATION.md.
- [ ] **F0032** Native DSD and verified bit-perfect exclusive output. — NativeDsd streams raw DSF/DSDIFF into ASIO packed DSD buffers without PCM/DoP; simulated callback byte integrity, bit order, bounds and malformed input tests pass. RealtimeAudioSession preserves direct integer WAV bytes and negotiates exact WASAPI exclusive formats. No compatible ASIO DAC is installed and external digital loopback proof remains pending. docs/REALTIME_AUDIO.md.

### Video and subtitles

- [ ] **F0085** Complete HDR/color management validation. — NativeGpuVideoWindow / NativeVideoSurface now provide a separate native Direct3D11 HWND path that bypasses the SDR WPF memory presenter. Actual 10-bit PQ/BT.2020 HEVC source, output startup, pause/resume, main Stop and disposal tested. DisplayColorInspector reads modern/legacy Windows Advanced Color, bit depth, encoding and legacy ICC metadata without changing OS state. LG HDR support is present but disabled; both active outputs report SDR/8-bit. Actual HDR swap-chain/colorimetric/ICC acceptance remains pending. docs/NATIVE_GPU_PRESENTATION.md.

### Network and distribution

- [ ] **F0091** Published releases, downloadable builds and release automation. — Local self-contained ZIP/MSIX packaging, SHA-256 manifest and manual release workflow added. Public publication is deferred by the user; no workflow/release was dispatched. docs/LOCAL_COMPLETION_PROGRESS.md.

No real certificate path was exercised. Signing/public distribution/installed update acceptance is deferred by the user. Local use needs no PFX. docs/LOCAL_COMPLETION_PROGRESS.md.

### Audio

- [ ] **F0106** Explicit 2.1/5.1/7.1 speaker layouts, arbitrary channel matrices, per-channel gain/delay and verified physical surround output. — Live WASAPI PCM now uses true WAVEFORMATEXTENSIBLE masks for mono/stereo/2.1/5.1(side)/7.1, rejects unsupported exact multichannel formats and supports arbitrary matrices/per-channel gain/delay. Thirty signal combinations and 60 format negotiations per actual endpoint are checked. Logical native 8x8/offline mastering retained. Physical speaker wiring/output certification remains pending. docs/REALTIME_AUDIO.md.

## Compatibility notes

Playback depends on the selected LibVLC runtime, plugins, input and hardware. Universal format support, native DSD, bit-perfect output and a complete hardware matrix are not claimed. The 15/20/31-band equalizer requires the native DSP module built from source; a legacy-only build retains ten-band processing. UI status identifies actual native acknowledgement.
